23	def check_hermes_available() -> tuple[bool, str]:
24	    """Check if Hermes Agent is importable and has API credentials."""
25	    try:
26	        from run_agent import AIAgent  # noqa: F401
27	    except ImportError:
28	        return (False, "hermes-agent not installed. Install with: pip install hermes-agent")
29	
30	    # Check for API key — Hermes stores keys in ~/.hermes/.env, loaded via dotenv.
31	    # After dotenv load, the key is available as an env var.
32	    api_key = os.environ.get("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
33	    if not api_key:
34	        # Try loading from Hermes .env file directly
35	        try:
36	            from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path
37	            env_path = get_env_path()
38	            if env_path and env_path.exists():
39	                for line in env_path.read_text().splitlines():
40	                    line = line.strip()
41	                    if line.startswith("OPENROUTER_API_KEY="):
42	                        api_key = line.split("=", 1)[1].strip().strip("'\"")
43	                        break
44	        except (ImportError, Exception):
45	            pass
46	
47	    if not api_key:
48	        return (False, "OPENROUTER_API_KEY not set. Configure via env var or ~/.hermes/.env")
49	    return (True, "")
50	
51	
52	def _toolsets_for_phase(phase: str) -> list[str] | None:
53	    """Return toolsets for a given megaplan phase.
54	
55	    Execute phase gets full terminal + file + web access.
56	    Plan, critique, and revise get file + web (verify APIs against docs).
57	    Gate, finalize, review get file only (judgment, not investigation).
58	    """
59	    if phase == "execute":
60	        return ["terminal", "file", "web"]
61	    if phase in ("plan", "prep", "critique", "revise"):
62	        return ["file", "web"]
63	    return ["file"]
64	
65	
66	_TEMPLATE_FILE_PHASES = {"finalize", "review", "prep"}
67	_CUSTOM_TEMPLATE_PHASES = {"critique", "review"}
68	
69	
70	def _template_has_content(payload: dict, step: str) -> bool:
71	    """Check if a template-file payload has real content (not just the empty template)."""
72	    if step == "critique":
73	        # For critique: check if any check has non-empty findings
74	        checks = payload.get("checks", [])
75	        if isinstance(checks, list):
76	            for check in checks:
77	                if isinstance(check, dict):
78	                    findings = check.get("findings", [])
79	                    if isinstance(findings, list) and findings:
80	                        return True
81	        # Also check flags array
82	        flags = payload.get("flags", [])
83	        if isinstance(flags, list) and flags:
84	            return True
85	        return False
86	    if step == "review":
87	        # For review: the template is pre-populated with task IDs and sense-check
88	        # IDs (empty verdicts). Check that at least one verdict was filled in, or
89	        # that summary/review_verdict has content.
90	        review_verdict = payload.get("review_verdict", "")
91	        if isinstance(review_verdict, str) and review_verdict.strip():
92	            return True
93	        summary = payload.get("summary", "")
94	        if isinstance(summary, str) and summary.strip():
95	            return True
96	        for tv in payload.get("task_verdicts", []):
97	            if isinstance(tv, dict) and tv.get("reviewer_verdict", "").strip():
98	                return True
99	        for sc in payload.get("sense_check_verdicts", []):
100	            if isinstance(sc, dict) and sc.get("verdict", "").strip():
101	                return True
102	        return False
103	    # For other phases: any non-empty array or non-empty string
104	    return any(
105	        (isinstance(v, list) and v) or (isinstance(v, str) and v.strip())
106	        for k, v in payload.items()
107	    )
108	
109	
110	def _build_output_template(step: str, schema: dict) -> str:
111	    """Build a JSON template from a schema for non-critique template-file phases."""
112	    return _schema_template(schema)
113	
114	
115	def parse_agent_output(
116	    agent,
117	    result: dict,
118	    *,
119	    output_path: Path | None,
120	    schema: dict,
121	    step: str,
122	    project_dir: Path,
123	    plan_dir: Path,
124	) -> tuple[dict, str]:
125	    """Parse a Hermes agent result into a structured payload."""
126	    raw_output = result.get("final_response", "") or ""
127	    messages = result.get("messages", [])
128	
129	    # If final_response is empty and the model used tools, the agent loop exited
130	    # after tool calls without giving the model a chance to output JSON.
131	    # Make one more API call with the template to force structured output.
132	    if not raw_output.strip() and messages and any(m.get("tool_calls") for m in messages if m.get("role") == "assistant"):
133	        try:
134	            template = _schema_template(schema)
135	            summary_prompt = (
136	                "You have finished investigating. Now fill in this JSON template with your findings "
137	                "and output it as your response. Output ONLY the raw JSON, nothing else.\n\n"
138	                + template
139	            )
140	            summary_result = agent.run_conversation(
141	                user_message=summary_prompt,
142	                conversation_history=messages,
143	            )
144	            raw_output = summary_result.get("final_response", "") or ""
145	            messages = summary_result.get("messages", messages)
146	            if raw_output.strip():
147	                print(f"[hermes-worker] Got JSON from template prompt ({len(raw_output)} chars)", file=sys.stderr)
148	        except Exception as exc:
149	            print(f"[hermes-worker] Template prompt failed: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
150	
151	    # For template-file phases, check the template file FIRST — we told the
152	    # model to write there, so it's the primary output path.
153	    payload = None
154	    if output_path and output_path.exists():
155	        try:
156	            candidate_payload = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
157	            if isinstance(candidate_payload, dict):
158	                # Check if the model actually filled in findings (not just the empty template)
159	                has_content = _template_has_content(candidate_payload, step)
160	                if has_content:
161	                    payload = candidate_payload
162	                    print(f"[hermes-worker] Read JSON from template file: {output_path}", file=sys.stderr)
163	                else:
164	                    print(f"[hermes-worker] Template file exists but has no real content", file=sys.stderr)
165	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
166	            pass
167	
168	    # Try parsing the final text response
169	    if payload is None:
170	        payload = _parse_json_response(raw_output)
171	
172	    # Fallback: some models (GLM-5) put JSON in reasoning/think tags
173	    # instead of content. Just grab it from there.
174	    if payload is None and messages:
175	        payload = _extract_json_from_reasoning(messages)
176	        if payload is not None:
177	            print(f"[hermes-worker] Extracted JSON from reasoning tags", file=sys.stderr)
178	
179	    # Fallback: check all assistant message content fields (not just final_response)
180	    # The model may have output JSON in an earlier message before making more tool calls
181	    if payload is None and messages:
182	        for msg in reversed(messages):
183	            if msg.get("role") != "assistant":
184	                continue
185	            content = msg.get("content", "")
186	            if isinstance(content, str) and content.strip():
187	                payload = _parse_json_response(content)
188	                if payload is not None:
189	                    print(f"[hermes-worker] Extracted JSON from assistant message content", file=sys.stderr)
190	                    break
191	
192	    # Fallback: for execute phase, reconstruct from tool calls + git diff
193	    if payload is None and step == "execute":
194	        payload = _reconstruct_execute_payload(messages, project_dir, plan_dir, mode=plan_mode)
195	        if payload is not None:
196	            print(f"[hermes-worker] Reconstructed execute payload from tool calls", file=sys.stderr)
197	
198	    # Fallback: the model may have written the JSON to a different file location
199	    if payload is None:
200	        schema_filename = STEP_SCHEMA_FILENAMES.get(step, f"{step}.json")
201	        for candidate in [
202	            plan_dir / f"{step}_output.json",  # template file path
203	            project_dir / schema_filename,
204	            plan_dir / schema_filename,
205	            project_dir / f"{step}.json",
206	        ]:
207	            if candidate.exists() and candidate != output_path:  # skip if already checked
208	                try:
209	                    payload = json.loads(candidate.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
210	                    print(f"[hermes-worker] Read JSON from file written by model: {candidate}", file=sys.stderr)
211	                    break
212	                except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
213	                    pass
214	
215	    # Last resort for template-file phases: the model investigated and produced
216	    # text findings but didn't write valid JSON anywhere. Ask it to restructure
217	    # its analysis into JSON. This catches MiniMax's pattern of outputting markdown.
218	    if payload is None and output_path and messages:
219	        try:
220	            template = _schema_template(schema)
221	            summary_prompt = (
222	                "You have completed your investigation but your findings were not written as JSON. "
223	                "Take everything you found and fill in this JSON template. "
224	                "Output ONLY the raw JSON, nothing else — no markdown, no explanation.\n\n"
225	                + template
226	            )
227	            print(f"[hermes-worker] Attempting summary prompt to extract JSON from investigation", file=sys.stderr)
228	            summary_result = agent.run_conversation(
229	                user_message=summary_prompt,
230	                conversation_history=messages,
231	            )
232	            summary_output = summary_result.get("final_response", "") or ""
233	            if summary_output.strip():
234	                payload = _parse_json_response(summary_output)
235	                if payload is not None:
236	                    print(f"[hermes-worker] Got JSON from summary prompt ({len(summary_output)} chars)", file=sys.stderr)
237	        except Exception as exc:
238	            print(f"[hermes-worker] Summary prompt failed: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
239	
240	    if payload is None:
241	        raise CliError(
242	            "worker_parse_error",
243	            f"Hermes worker returned invalid JSON for step '{step}': "
244	            f"could not extract JSON from response ({len(raw_output)} chars)",
245	            extra={"raw_output": raw_output},
246	        )
247	
248	    result["final_response"] = raw_output
249	    result["messages"] = messages
250	    return payload, raw_output

253	def clean_parsed_payload(payload: dict, schema: dict, step: str) -> None:
254	    """Normalize a parsed Hermes payload before validation."""
255	    # Strip guide-only fields from critique checks (guidance/prior_findings
256	    # are in the template file to help the model, but not part of the schema)
257	    if step == "critique" and isinstance(payload.get("checks"), list):
258	        for check in payload["checks"]:
259	            if isinstance(check, dict):
260	                check.pop("guidance", None)
261	                check.pop("prior_findings", None)
262	
263	    # Fill in missing required fields with safe defaults before validation.
264	    # Models often omit empty arrays/strings that megaplan requires.
265	    _fill_schema_defaults(payload, schema)
266	
267	    # Normalize field aliases in nested arrays (e.g. critique flags use
268	    # "summary" instead of "concern", "detail" instead of "evidence").
269	    _normalize_nested_aliases(payload, schema)

272	def run_hermes_step(
273	    step: str,
274	    state: PlanState,
275	    plan_dir: Path,
276	    *,
277	    root: Path,
278	    fresh: bool,
279	    model: str | None = None,
280	    prompt_override: str | None = None,
281	) -> WorkerResult:
282	    """Run a megaplan phase using Hermes Agent via OpenRouter.
283	
284	    Structured output is enforced via the prompt (megaplan prompts already
285	    embed the JSON schema). The final response is parsed and validated.
286	    """
287	    if os.getenv(MOCK_ENV_VAR) == "1":
288	        return mock_worker_output(step, state, plan_dir, prompt_override=prompt_override)
289	
290	    from run_agent import AIAgent
291	    from hermes_state import SessionDB
292	
293	    project_dir = Path(state["config"]["project_dir"])
294	    plan_mode = state["config"].get("mode", "code")
295	    from megaplan.schemas import get_execution_schema_key
296	    schema_name = get_execution_schema_key(plan_mode) if step == "execute" else STEP_SCHEMA_FILENAMES[step]
297	    schema = read_json(schemas_root(root) / schema_name)
298	    output_path: Path | None = None
299	
300	    # Session management
301	    session_key = session_key_for(step, "hermes", model=model)
302	    session = state["sessions"].get(session_key, {})
303	    session_id = session.get("id") if not fresh else None
304	
305	    # Reload conversation history for session continuity
306	    conversation_history = None
307	    if session_id:
308	        try:
309	            db = SessionDB()
310	            conversation_history = db.get_messages_as_conversation(session_id)
311	        except Exception:
312	            conversation_history = None
313	
314	    # Generate new session ID if needed
315	    if not session_id:
316	        import uuid
317	        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
318	
319	    # Build prompt — megaplan prompts embed the JSON schema, but some models
320	    # ignore formatting instructions buried in long prompts.  Append a clear
321	    # reminder so the final response is valid JSON, not markdown.
322	    prompt = prompt_override if prompt_override is not None else create_hermes_prompt(
323	        step, state, plan_dir, root=root
324	    )
325	    # Add web search guidance for phases that have it
326	    if step in ("plan", "critique", "revise"):
327	        prompt += (
328	            "\n\nWEB SEARCH: You have web_search and web_extract tools. "
329	            "If the task involves a framework API you're not certain about — "
330	            "for example a specific Next.js feature, a particular import path, "
331	            "or a config flag that might have changed between versions — "
332	            "search for the current documentation before committing to an approach. "
333	            "Your training data may be outdated for newer framework features."
334	        )
335	    elif step == "execute":
336	        prompt += (
337	            "\n\nWEB SEARCH: You have web_search available. "
338	            "If you encounter an API you're not sure about while coding, "
339	            "search before writing — a quick lookup is cheaper than a build failure."
340	            "\n\nIMPORTANT: Do NOT rename, modify, or delete EVAL.ts or any test files. "
341	            "They are used for scoring after execution and must remain unchanged."
342	        )
343	
344	    # Critique and review: use custom template writers that pre-populate IDs.
345	    # Other template-file phases: hermes_worker writes a generic template.
346	    if step == "critique":
347	        output_path = plan_dir / "critique_output.json"
348	    elif step == "review":
349	        from megaplan.prompts.review import _write_review_template
350	        output_path = _write_review_template(plan_dir, state)
351	        prompt += (
352	            f"\n\nOUTPUT FILE: {output_path}\n"
353	            "This file is your ONLY output. It contains a JSON template PRE-POPULATED with "
354	            "the task IDs and sense-check IDs you must review.\n"
355	            "Workflow:\n"
356	            "1. Read the file to see all the task IDs and sense-check IDs\n"
357	            "2. Investigate each task — cross-reference executor claims against the git diff\n"
358	            "3. Fill in every reviewer_verdict, evidence_files, verdict, criteria, and summary\n"
359	            "4. Write the completed JSON back to the file\n\n"
360	            "CRITICAL: You MUST fill in ALL task_verdicts and sense_check_verdicts entries. "
361	            "Do NOT leave reviewer_verdict or verdict fields empty. "
362	            "Do NOT put your results in a text response. The file is the only output that matters."
363	        )
364	    elif step in _TEMPLATE_FILE_PHASES:
365	        output_path = plan_dir / f"{step}_output.json"
366	        output_path.write_text(
367	            _build_output_template(step, schema),
368	            encoding="utf-8",
369	        )
370	        prompt += (
371	            f"\n\nOUTPUT FILE: {output_path}\n"
372	            "This file is your ONLY output. It contains a JSON template with the structure to fill in.\n"
373	            "Workflow:\n"
374	            "1. Start by reading the file to see the structure\n"
375	            "2. Do your work\n"
376	            "3. Read the file, add your results, write it back\n\n"
377	            "Do NOT put your results in a text response. The file is the only output that matters."
378	        )
379	    else:
380	        template = _schema_template(schema)
381	        prompt += (
382	            "\n\nIMPORTANT: Your final response MUST be a single valid JSON object. "
383	            "Do NOT use markdown. Do NOT wrap in code fences. Output ONLY raw JSON "
384	            "matching this template:\n\n" + template
385	        )
386	
387	    # Redirect stdout to stderr before creating the agent.  Hermes's
388	    # KawaiiSpinner captures sys.stdout at __init__ time and writes
389	    # directly to it (bypassing _print_fn).  By swapping stdout to
390	    # stderr here, all spinner/progress output flows to stderr while
391	    # megaplan keeps stdout clean for structured JSON results.
392	    real_stdout = sys.stdout
393	    sys.stdout = sys.stderr
394	
395	    # Resolve model provider — support direct API providers via prefix
396	    # e.g. "zhipu:glm-5.1" → base_url=Zhipu API, model="glm-5.1"
397	    # Uses the key pool for key rotation and cooldown on 429s.
398	    from megaplan.key_pool import resolve_model as _resolve_model, acquire_key, report_429
399	    resolved_model, agent_kwargs = _resolve_model(model)
400	
401	    toolsets = _toolsets_for_phase(step)
402	
403	    # Disable reasoning/thinking for models that default to reasoning mode.
404	    # When reasoning is enabled, some models (Qwen3.5, DeepSeek-R1) put all
405	    # output in the reasoning field and return content: null.  Megaplan needs
406	    # structured JSON in the content field, so force reasoning off for any
407	    # model family known to use reasoning by default on OpenRouter.
408	    _model_lower = (resolved_model or "").lower()
409	    _reasoning_families = ("qwen/qwen3", "deepseek/deepseek-r1")
410	    _reasoning_off = (
411	        {"enabled": False}
412	        if any(_model_lower.startswith(prefix) for prefix in _reasoning_families)
413	        else None
414	    )
415	
416	    def _make_agent(agent_model: str, extra_kwargs: dict) -> "AIAgent":
417	        current_agent = AIAgent(
418	            model=agent_model,
419	            quiet_mode=True,
420	            skip_context_files=True,
421	            skip_memory=True,
422	            enabled_toolsets=toolsets,
423	            session_id=session_id,
424	            session_db=SessionDB(),
425	            # Cap output tokens to prevent repetition loops (Qwen generates 330K+
426	            # of repeated text without a limit). 8192 is plenty for any megaplan
427	            # phase response. Execute gets more since it may include verbose output.
428	            max_tokens=16384 if step == "execute" else 8192,
429	            reasoning_config=_reasoning_off,
430	            **extra_kwargs,
431	        )
432	        current_agent._print_fn = lambda *a, **kw: print(*a, **kw, file=sys.stderr)
433	        if not toolsets:
434	            current_agent.set_response_format(schema, name=f"megaplan_{step}")
435	        return current_agent
436	
437	    def _rewrite_output_template(current_output_path: Path | None) -> Path | None:
438	        if current_output_path is None:
439	            return None
440	        if step == "critique":
441	            from megaplan._core import configured_robustness
442	            from megaplan.audits.robustness import checks_for_robustness
443	            from megaplan.prompts import _write_critique_template
444	
445	            return _write_critique_template(
446	                plan_dir,
447	                state,
448	                checks_for_robustness(configured_robustness(state)),
449	            )
450	        if step == "review":
451	            from megaplan.prompts.review import _write_review_template
452	            return _write_review_template(plan_dir, state)
453	        current_output_path.write_text(
454	            _build_output_template(step, schema),
455	            encoding="utf-8",
456	        )
457	        return current_output_path
458	
459	    def _failure_reason(exc: Exception) -> str:
460	        if isinstance(exc, CliError):
461	            return exc.message
462	        return str(exc) or exc.__class__.__name__
463	
464	    def _run_attempt(current_agent, current_output_path: Path | None) -> tuple[dict, dict, str]:
465	        current_result = current_agent.run_conversation(
466	            user_message=prompt,
467	            conversation_history=conversation_history,
468	        )
469	        current_payload, current_raw_output = parse_agent_output(
470	            current_agent,
471	            current_result,
472	            output_path=current_output_path,
473	            schema=schema,
474	            step=step,
475	            project_dir=project_dir,
476	            plan_dir=plan_dir,
477	        )
478	        clean_parsed_payload(current_payload, schema, step)
479	        messages = current_result.get("messages", [])
480	
481	        try:
482	            validate_payload(step, current_payload)
483	        except CliError as error:
484	            # For execute, try reconstructed payload if validation fails
485	            if step == "execute":
486	                reconstructed = _reconstruct_execute_payload(messages, project_dir, plan_dir, mode=plan_mode)
487	                if reconstructed is not None:
488	                    try:
489	                        validate_payload(step, reconstructed)
490	                        current_payload = reconstructed
491	                        print(
492	                            "[hermes-worker] Using reconstructed payload (original failed validation)",
493	                            file=sys.stderr,
494	                        )
495	                        error = None
496	                    except CliError:
497	                        pass
498	            if error is not None:
499	                raise CliError(error.code, error.message, extra={"raw_output": current_raw_output}) from error
500	
501	        return current_result, current_payload, current_raw_output
502	
503	    agent = _make_agent(resolved_model, agent_kwargs)
504	    # Don't set response_format when tools are enabled — many models
505	    # (Qwen, GLM-5) hang or produce garbage when both are active.
506	    # The JSON template in the prompt is sufficient; _parse_json_response
507	    # handles code fences and markdown wrapping.
508	
509	    # Run — with fallback to OpenRouter for MiniMax if primary API fails
510	    started = time.monotonic()
511	    try:
512	        try:
513	            result, payload, raw_output = _run_attempt(agent, output_path)
514	        except Exception as exc:
515	            # Report 429 to key pool so it cools down this key
516	            exc_str = str(exc)
517	            if "429" in exc_str:
518	                if model and model.startswith("minimax:"):
519	                    report_429("minimax", agent_kwargs.get("api_key", ""), cooldown_secs=60)
520	                elif model and model.startswith("zhipu:"):
521	                    # Quota exhaustion needs a long cooldown (hours, not seconds)
522	                    cooldown = 3600 if "Limit Exhausted" in exc_str else 120
523	                    report_429("zhipu", agent_kwargs.get("api_key", ""), cooldown_secs=cooldown)
524	                    print(f"[hermes-worker] Z.AI key cooled down for {cooldown}s", file=sys.stderr)
525	            if model and model.startswith("minimax:"):
526	                or_key = acquire_key("openrouter")
527	                if or_key:
528	                    if isinstance(exc, CliError):
529	                        print(
530	                            f"[hermes-worker] MiniMax returned bad content ({_failure_reason(exc)}), falling back to OpenRouter",
531	                            file=sys.stderr,
532	                        )
533	                    else:
534	                        print(f"[hermes-worker] MiniMax failed ({exc}), falling back to OpenRouter", file=sys.stderr)
535	                    from megaplan.key_pool import minimax_openrouter_model
536	                    fallback_model = minimax_openrouter_model(model[len("minimax:"):])
537	                    output_path = _rewrite_output_template(output_path)
538	                    agent = _make_agent(
539	                        fallback_model,
540	                        {
541	                            "base_url": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
542	                            "api_key": or_key,
543	                        },
544	                    )
545	                    try:
546	                        result, payload, raw_output = _run_attempt(agent, output_path)
547	                    except Exception as fallback_exc:
548	                        raise CliError(
549	                            "worker_error",
550	                            (
551	                                f"Hermes worker failed for step '{step}' "
552	                                f"(both MiniMax and OpenRouter): primary={_failure_reason(exc)}; "
553	                                f"fallback={_failure_reason(fallback_exc)}"
554	                            ),
555	                            extra={"session_id": session_id},
556	                        ) from fallback_exc
557	                elif isinstance(exc, CliError):
558	                    raise
559	                else:
560	                    raise CliError(
561	                        "worker_error",
562	                        f"Hermes worker failed for step '{step}': {exc}",
563	                        extra={"session_id": session_id},
564	                    ) from exc
565	            elif isinstance(exc, CliError):
566	                raise
567	            else:
568	                raise CliError(
569	                    "worker_error",
570	                    f"Hermes worker failed for step '{step}': {exc}",
571	                    extra={"session_id": session_id},
572	                ) from exc
573	    finally:
574	        sys.stdout = real_stdout
575	    elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000)
576	
577	    cost_usd = result.get("estimated_cost_usd", 0.0) or 0.0
578	    prompt_tokens = int(result.get("prompt_tokens", 0) or 0)
579	    completion_tokens = int(result.get("completion_tokens", 0) or 0)
580	    total_tokens = int(result.get("total_tokens", 0) or 0)
581	
582	    return WorkerResult(
583	        payload=payload,
584	        raw_output=raw_output,
585	        duration_ms=elapsed_ms,
586	        cost_usd=float(cost_usd),
587	        session_id=session_id,
588	        prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
589	        completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
590	        total_tokens=total_tokens,
591	    )

594	def _extract_json_from_reasoning(messages: list) -> dict | None:
595	    """Extract JSON from the last assistant message's reasoning field.
596	
597	    Some models (GLM-5) wrap their entire response in think/reasoning tags,
598	    so the content field is empty but reasoning contains valid JSON.
599	    """
600	    for msg in reversed(messages):
601	        if msg.get("role") != "assistant":
602	            continue
603	        for field in ("reasoning", "reasoning_content"):
604	            text = msg.get(field)
605	            if isinstance(text, str) and text.strip():
606	                result = _parse_json_response(text)
607	                if result is not None:
608	                    return result
609	        # Also check reasoning_details (list of dicts with "content")
610	        details = msg.get("reasoning_details")
611	        if isinstance(details, list):
612	            for item in details:
613	                if isinstance(item, dict):
614	                    text = item.get("content", "")
615	                    if isinstance(text, str) and text.strip():
616	                        result = _parse_json_response(text)
617	                        if result is not None:
618	                            return result
619	    return None
620	
621	
622	def _reconstruct_execute_payload(
623	    messages: list,
624	    project_dir: Path,
625	    plan_dir: Path,
626	    *,
627	    mode: str = "code",
628	) -> dict | None:
629	    """Reconstruct an execute phase response from tool calls and git state.
630	
631	    When the model did the work via tools but couldn't produce the JSON
632	    report (e.g., response trapped in think tags, or timeout), build the
633	    response from what actually happened.
634	    """
635	    import subprocess
636	
637	    # Collect tool calls from messages
638	    tool_calls = []
639	    files_changed = set()
640	    commands_run = []
641	    for msg in messages:
642	        if msg.get("role") != "assistant":
643	            continue
644	        for tc in msg.get("tool_calls") or []:
645	            fn = tc.get("function", {})
646	            if not isinstance(fn, dict):
647	                continue
648	            name = fn.get("name", "")
649	            args_raw = fn.get("arguments", "{}")
650	            try:
651	                args = json.loads(args_raw) if isinstance(args_raw, str) else args_raw
652	            except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
653	                args = {}
654	            if not isinstance(args, dict):
655	                args = {}
656	
657	            tool_calls.append({"name": name, "args": args})
658	
659	            if name in ("write_file", "patch", "edit_file", "apply_patch"):
660	                path = args.get("path", "")
661	                if isinstance(path, str) and path:
662	                    try:
663	                        rel = str(Path(path).relative_to(project_dir))
664	                    except ValueError:
665	                        rel = path
666	                    files_changed.add(rel)
667	            elif name in ("terminal", "shell"):
668	                cmd = args.get("command", "")
669	                if isinstance(cmd, str) and cmd:
670	                    commands_run.append(cmd)
671	
672	    if mode != "doc":
673	        try:
674	            diff_result = subprocess.run(
675	                ["git", "diff", "--name-only", "HEAD"],
676	                cwd=project_dir,
677	                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=False,
678	            )
679	            if diff_result.returncode == 0:
680	                for line in diff_result.stdout.splitlines():
681	                    if line.strip():
682	                        files_changed.add(line.strip())
683	        except Exception:
684	            pass
685	
686	        try:
687	            status_result = subprocess.run(
688	                ["git", "status", "--porcelain"],
689	                cwd=project_dir,
690	                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=False,
691	            )
692	            if status_result.returncode == 0:
693	                for line in status_result.stdout.splitlines():
694	                    if line.startswith("?? ") or line.startswith("A  ") or line.startswith("M  "):
695	                        fname = line[3:].strip()
696	                        if fname and not fname.startswith(".megaplan/"):
697	                            files_changed.add(fname)
698	        except Exception:
699	            pass
700	
701	    if not tool_calls and not files_changed:
702	        return None
703	
704	    # Try to read checkpoint file for task updates
705	    task_updates = []
706	    checkpoint_files = sorted(plan_dir.glob("execution_batch_*.json"), reverse=True)
707	    for cp_file in checkpoint_files:
708	        try:
709	            cp_data = json.loads(cp_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
710	            updates = cp_data.get("task_updates", [])
711	            if isinstance(updates, list):
712	                task_updates.extend(updates)
713	        except Exception:
714	            pass
715	
716	    if mode == "doc":
717	        sections_written = sorted(
718	            {
719	                section
720	                for tu in task_updates
721	                for section in tu.get("sections_written", [])
722	                if isinstance(section, str) and section.strip()
723	            }
724	        )
725	        return {
726	            "output": f"[Reconstructed from tool calls] Made {len(tool_calls)} tool calls, wrote {len(sections_written)} sections.",
727	            "sections_written": sections_written,
728	            "commands_run": [],
729	            "deviations": ["Execute response reconstructed from tool calls — model failed to produce JSON report."],
730	            "task_updates": task_updates,
731	            "sense_check_acknowledgments": [],
732	        }
733	
734	    files_list = sorted(files_changed)
735	    return {
736	        "output": f"[Reconstructed from tool calls] Made {len(tool_calls)} tool calls, changed {len(files_list)} files.",
737	        "files_changed": files_list,
738	        "commands_run": commands_run,
739	        "deviations": ["Execute response reconstructed from tool calls — model failed to produce JSON report."],
740	        "task_updates": task_updates,
741	        "sense_check_acknowledgments": [],
742	    }
743	
744	
745	def _fill_schema_defaults(payload: dict, schema: dict) -> None:
746	    """Fill missing required fields with safe defaults based on schema types.
747	
748	    Models often omit empty arrays, empty strings, or optional-sounding fields
749	    that the schema marks as required. Rather than rejecting the response,
750	    fill them with type-appropriate defaults.
751	    """
752	    required = schema.get("required", [])
753	    properties = schema.get("properties", {})
754	    for field in required:
755	        if field in payload:
756	            continue
757	        prop = properties.get(field, {})
758	        ptype = prop.get("type", "string")
759	        if ptype == "array":
760	            payload[field] = []
761	        elif ptype == "object":
762	            payload[field] = {}
763	        elif ptype == "boolean":
764	            payload[field] = False
765	        elif ptype in ("number", "integer"):
766	            payload[field] = 0
767	        else:
768	            payload[field] = ""
769	
770	
771	def _normalize_nested_aliases(payload: dict, schema: dict) -> None:
772	    """Normalize field aliases in nested array items.
773	
774	    Models often use synonyms for required fields (e.g. "summary" instead of
775	    "concern", "detail" instead of "evidence"). This applies the alias mapping
776	    from merge._FIELD_ALIASES to nested objects in arrays.
777	    """
778	    from megaplan.execute.merge import _FIELD_ALIASES
779	
780	    properties = schema.get("properties", {})
781	    for field, prop in properties.items():
782	        if prop.get("type") != "array" or field not in payload:
783	            continue
784	        items_schema = prop.get("items", {})
785	        if items_schema.get("type") != "object":
786	            continue
787	        required = items_schema.get("required", [])
788	        items = payload[field]
789	        if not isinstance(items, list):
790	            continue
791	        for item in items:
792	            if not isinstance(item, dict):
793	                continue
794	            for req_field in required:
795	                if req_field in item and item[req_field]:
796	                    continue  # Already has a non-empty value
797	                aliases = _FIELD_ALIASES.get(req_field, ())
798	                for alias in aliases:
799	                    if alias in item and item[alias]:
800	                        item[req_field] = item[alias]
801	                        break
802	
803	
804	def _schema_template(schema: dict) -> str:
805	    """Generate a JSON template from a schema showing required keys with placeholder values."""
806	    props = schema.get("properties", {})
807	    if not isinstance(props, dict):
808	        return "{}"
809	    template = {}
810	    for key, prop in props.items():
811	        if not isinstance(prop, dict):
812	            template[key] = "..."
813	            continue
814	        ptype = prop.get("type", "string")
815	        if ptype == "string":
816	            desc = prop.get("description", "")
817	            template[key] = f"<{desc}>" if desc else "..."
818	        elif ptype == "array":
819	            items = prop.get("items", {})
820	            if isinstance(items, dict) and items.get("type") == "string":
821	                template[key] = ["..."]
822	            else:
823	                template[key] = []
824	        elif ptype == "boolean":
825	            template[key] = True
826	        elif ptype in ("number", "integer"):
827	            template[key] = 0
828	        elif ptype == "object":
829	            template[key] = {}
830	        else:
831	            template[key] = "..."
832	    return json.dumps(template, indent=2)
833	
834	
835	def _parse_json_response(text: str) -> dict | None:
836	    """Extract a JSON object from a model response.
837	
838	    Tries in order:
839	    1. Direct JSON parse
840	    2. Repair common JSON issues (escaped newlines in structural positions)
841	    3. Extract from ```json ... ``` code block
842	    4. Find first { ... } JSON object in the text
843	
844	    Each step also tries the repaired version.
845	    """
846	    text = text.strip()
847	    if not text:
848	        return None
849	
850	    for candidate in [text, _repair_json(text)]:
851	        # Direct parse
852	        try:
853	            parsed = json.loads(candidate)
854	            if isinstance(parsed, dict):
855	                return parsed
856	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
857	            pass
858	
859	        # Extract from code block
860	        import re
861	        code_block = re.search(r'```(?:json)?\s*\n(.*?)\n```', candidate, re.DOTALL)
862	        if code_block:
863	            block_text = code_block.group(1)
864	            for block_candidate in [block_text, _repair_json(block_text)]:
865	                try:
866	                    parsed = json.loads(block_candidate)
867	                    if isinstance(parsed, dict):
868	                        return parsed
869	                except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
870	                    pass
871	
872	        # Find first JSON object
873	        decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
874	        for i, ch in enumerate(candidate):
875	            if ch != '{':
876	                continue
877	            try:
878	                parsed, end = decoder.raw_decode(candidate[i:])
879	                if isinstance(parsed, dict):
880	                    return parsed
881	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
882	                continue
883	
884	    return None
885	
886	
887	def _repair_json(text: str) -> str:
888	    """Fix common JSON issues from LLM output.
889	
890	    Models sometimes mix escaped and literal newlines, or produce
891	    backslash-n outside of strings where real whitespace is needed.
892	    """
893	    # Replace literal \n that appear outside of JSON strings with actual newlines.
894	    # This handles the case where the model outputs [\n    "item"] instead of
895	    # [\n    "item"] — the \n is structural whitespace, not string content.
896	    result = []
897	    in_string = False
898	    escape = False
899	    i = 0
900	    while i < len(text):
901	        ch = text[i]
902	        if escape:
903	            result.append(ch)
904	            escape = False
905	            i += 1
906	            continue
907	        if ch == '\\' and in_string:
908	            escape = True
909	            result.append(ch)
910	            i += 1
911	            continue
912	        if ch == '"' and not escape:
913	            in_string = not in_string
914	            result.append(ch)
915	            i += 1
916	            continue
917	        # Outside a string, replace \n with actual newline
918	        if not in_string and ch == '\\' and i + 1 < len(text) and text[i + 1] == 'n':
919	            result.append('\n')
920	            i += 2
921	            continue
922	        result.append(ch)
923	        i += 1
924	    return ''.join(result)
925

I have enough to write the plan. Key findings from the megaplan side:

- `from run_agent import AIAgent` and `from hermes_state import SessionDB` appear in: `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81, 209-210`, `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52`.
- `import run_agent` (availability probe): `megaplan/workers.py:1580` and `megaplan/_core/io.py:270`.
- `check_hermes_available()` callers: `megaplan/workers.py:1649, 1655` (both in `resolve_agent_mode`); it uses `hermes_cli.config.get_env_path` which is in the delete list.
- Tests mock these import paths: `tests/test_parallel_critique.py:404-407`, `tests/test_parallel_review.py:198-201, 281-284`.
- README references `pip install hermes-agent` (line 23) and `~/.hermes/.env` (lines 25, 83).
- All `run_agent`/`hermes_state`/`hermes_cli` imports in `megaplan/` source are lazy (inside functions), so `import megaplan.hermes_worker` does not require runtime deps.
- I cannot list `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/` directly (outside working-dir sandbox), so internal module reachability will be mapped *after* the subtree merge lands inside `megaplan/agent/`.

# Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor the local `hermes-agent` repo into `megaplan` as an in-tree subpackage at `megaplan/agent/` using `git subtree` (preserving history), then rewire the five megaplan call sites that currently import from the top-level `run_agent` / `hermes_state` / `hermes_cli` modules. Delete the dead-weight directories/files listed in the brief, remove the `check_hermes_available()` gating, and prove that (a) every megaplan module still imports and (b) `megaplan.agent.run_agent.AIAgent` + `megaplan.agent.hermes_state.SessionDB` resolve.

Out of scope for Job A (deferred to Job B): filtering `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter`, `acp_registry`, `environments/`. Those directories remain on disk; we only fix their import paths enough that `run_agent` / `hermes_state` can still be imported.

Current megaplan hermes touch surface (reachable-module audit already run):

| Site | Line | Symbol |
|---|---|---|
| `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` | 26, 36, 290-291 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB`, `hermes_cli.config.get_env_path` |
| `megaplan/review/parallel.py` | 80-81, 209-210 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB` |
| `megaplan/parallel_critique.py` | 51-52 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB` |
| `megaplan/workers.py` | 1580, 1649-1656 | `import run_agent` probe, `check_hermes_available()` |
| `megaplan/_core/io.py` | 270 | `import run_agent` probe |
| `tests/test_parallel_critique.py` | 404-407 | `sys.modules["run_agent"/"hermes_state"]` fakes |
| `tests/test_parallel_review.py` | 198-201, 281-284 | same |
| `README.md` | 23, 25, 83 | install doc |

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Pre-flight — clean working tree and snapshot hermes HEAD (`git`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Stash or commit** the three dirty files (`megaplan/execute/core.py`, `megaplan/prompts/execute_doc.py`, `megaplan/prompts/planning.py`) so the subtree merge starts from a clean tree.
2. **Record** the hermes-agent source commit: `git -C /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent rev-parse HEAD` and its default branch. Note it in the commit message for provenance.
3. **Confirm** default branch name (assumption: `main`; fall back to `master`).

### Step 2: Subtree-merge hermes-agent at `megaplan/agent/` (`git subtree`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** (from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan`):
   ```bash
   git subtree add --prefix=megaplan/agent /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent main
   ```
   No `--squash` — brief requires preserving history. This produces a merge commit that joins the two histories.
2. **Verify** `megaplan/agent/run_agent.py` and `megaplan/agent/hermes_state.py` now exist.
3. **Add** `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` (empty, or re-exporting `AIAgent`/`SessionDB` — see Step 4).

### Step 3: Map reachable-module surface inside the vendored tree (`megaplan/agent/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Parse** `megaplan/agent/run_agent.py` and `megaplan/agent/hermes_state.py` imports with `grep -nE "^(from|import) " megaplan/agent/run_agent.py megaplan/agent/hermes_state.py`.
2. **Transitively walk** the graph (breadth-first) until you reach only stdlib + third-party packages. Record the reachable-in-tree module list — this becomes the "must-import-cleanly" set for Job A.
3. **Tag** any module that transitively pulls in `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter`, `acp_registry`, or `environments/`. Those pulls remain but their internal absolute imports (e.g. `from tools.x import ...`) must be rewritten to `from megaplan.agent.tools.x import ...` for importability. Contents of those dirs stay untouched (Job B).

### Step 4: Rewrite internal imports inside `megaplan/agent/` (`megaplan/agent/**/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Sweep** `megaplan/agent/` for absolute imports that refer to sibling modules now living under `megaplan.agent.*`:
   - `from run_agent import X` → `from megaplan.agent.run_agent import X`
   - `from hermes_state import X` → `from megaplan.agent.hermes_state import X`
   - `from tools...`, `from skills...`, `from acp_adapter...`, `from acp_registry...`, `from environments...` → `from megaplan.agent.<same> import ...`
   - `import run_agent` → `from megaplan.agent import run_agent`
   Restrict the rewrite to modules reachable from Step 3 plus any module the reachable set transitively imports. Leave test-only / example files alone if they're slated for deletion in Step 6.
2. **Decide** the `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` surface. Simplest: make it empty and let callers import submodules. If there is strong preference for a short import path, re-export `AIAgent` and `SessionDB`:
   ```python
   from megaplan.agent.run_agent import AIAgent
   from megaplan.agent.hermes_state import SessionDB
   ```
   (Assumption: leave `__init__.py` empty in Job A; keep per-module imports explicit at call sites for grep-ability.)

### Step 5: Rewire the five megaplan call sites (`megaplan/*.py`, `tests/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Edit** the five source sites to use the vendored path:
   - `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291` — `from megaplan.agent.run_agent import AIAgent` / `from megaplan.agent.hermes_state import SessionDB`.
   - `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81, 209-210` — same rewrite.
   - `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52` — same rewrite.
   - `megaplan/workers.py:1580` — change probe to `from megaplan.agent import run_agent`.
   - `megaplan/_core/io.py:270` — same probe rewrite.
2. **Delete** `check_hermes_available()` entirely from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` (lines 23-49) and **remove both call sites** in `megaplan/workers.py:1649-1658`. The surrounding fallback logic (agent_not_found branch) collapses to `raise CliError("agent_not_found", f"Agent '{agent}' not found")` since hermes is now always importable from the vendored tree. Drop the `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` import along with its function.
3. **Update tests** to mock the new paths:
   - `tests/test_parallel_critique.py:404-407` — mock `sys.modules["megaplan.agent.run_agent"]` and `sys.modules["megaplan.agent.hermes_state"]` instead.
   - `tests/test_parallel_review.py:198-201, 281-284` — same.
   Note: the call sites now use *absolute* imports (`from megaplan.agent.run_agent import AIAgent`), which resolves the module normally because the vendored package is real — the tests can actually drop the module-fakery and replace it with `monkeypatch.setattr(megaplan.agent.run_agent, "AIAgent", FakeAIAgent)`. Prefer that simpler form unless it breaks isolation.

### Step 6: Delete dead-weight paths inside `megaplan/agent/` (`git rm`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** a single `git rm -r` over the explicit brief list, relative to `megaplan/agent/`:
   ```
   evals/ landingpage/ website/ demo/ assets/ node_modules/
   package.json package-lock.json
   tinker-atropos/ honcho_integration/ mini-swe-agent/ gateway/ cron/ hermes_cli/
   cli.py auto_improve/ batch_runner.py rl_cli.py
   datagen-config-examples/
   *.json   (root-level benchmark artifacts only — NOT nested configs)
   RELEASE_v*.md setup-hermes.sh cli-config.yaml.example
   ```
   Use `git ls-files megaplan/agent/*.json` to enumerate root-level JSON before removal so you don't nuke a required in-tree config.
2. **Leave** `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `environments/` (Job B handles those).
3. **Spot-check** with `ls megaplan/agent/` that the kept surface is approximately: `run_agent.py`, `hermes_state.py`, `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `environments/`, `__init__.py`, and whatever reachable modules Step 3 identified.

### Step 7: Update packaging + README (`pyproject.toml`, `README.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `megaplan/agent` to the wheel via the existing `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel] packages = ["megaplan"]` — already covered since `megaplan/agent` is a sub-package, but confirm no exclude pattern drops it.
2. **Update** README install line from `pip install megaplan-harness hermes-agent` to `pip install megaplan-harness` (hermes is now vendored). Keep the `~/.hermes/.env` config note — the agent still reads keys from there at runtime. If the `.env` path is looked up by the vendored `hermes_cli.config` module that we just deleted, grep the vendored tree for `get_env_path`; if it moved, no-op. If nothing else defines it, the env-var fallback (`OPENROUTER_API_KEY`) still works and the README note is accurate.
3. **Do NOT** add hermes-agent runtime deps (openai, httpx, etc.) to `pyproject.toml` in this job — per assumption below. Flag as a follow-up.

### Step 8: Verify imports and run the suite
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap import probe** (should succeed with only megaplan's current deps installed, since all hermes imports in megaplan are lazy):
   ```bash
   python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io"
   ```
2. **Vendored-package probe** (needs hermes runtime deps installed in the venv):
   ```bash
   python -c "from megaplan.agent.run_agent import AIAgent; from megaplan.agent.hermes_state import SessionDB"
   ```
   If this fails purely on missing third-party deps (e.g. `ModuleNotFoundError: openai`), document the required pip list and stop — runtime-dep consolidation is a follow-up.
3. **Grep** for stale references: `grep -rE "^( *)(from (run_agent|hermes_state|hermes_cli)|import (run_agent|hermes_state|hermes_cli))" megaplan/ tests/` → must return zero hits outside `megaplan/agent/`.
4. **Grep** for `check_hermes_available` anywhere in `megaplan/` and `tests/` → must return zero.
5. **Run** `pytest tests/` and confirm green, updating any further mocks the rewire surfaced.
6. **Smoke** `megaplan --help` to confirm CLI still loads.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree, record hermes HEAD (Step 1) — prerequisite to a clean subtree merge.
2. Subtree merge (Step 2) — creates the files before any rewrite needs them.
3. Map the reachable graph (Step 3) — informs the rewrite scope in Step 4.
4. Rewrite internal imports (Step 4) — must happen before external call-site rewire so the vendored package actually imports.
5. Rewire megaplan call sites + strip gating (Step 5).
6. Delete dead weight (Step 6) — safe only after reachable graph is known (Step 3).
7. Packaging + README (Step 7).
8. Validate (Step 8).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap: grep for stale import paths and `check_hermes_available`.
2. Cheap: bare-Python import probe of megaplan modules (no hermes runtime deps required).
3. Targeted: `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py` (the tests we touched).
4. Broad: full `pytest tests/`.
5. Integration (info-only): one real `megaplan` phase run against hermes once runtime deps are installed.

        # Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor the local `hermes-agent` repo into `megaplan` as an in-tree subpackage at `megaplan/agent/` using `git subtree` (preserving history), rewire the five megaplan call sites that import from top-level `run_agent` / `hermes_state` / `hermes_cli`, delete the dead-weight paths listed in the brief, remove `check_hermes_available()` gating, and prove that (a) every megaplan module still imports and (b) `megaplan.agent.run_agent.AIAgent` + `megaplan.agent.hermes_state.SessionDB` resolve. `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter`, `acp_registry`, `environments/` remain on disk — deferred to Job B; Job A only rewrites their internal absolute imports enough to keep the package importable.

Reachable-surface audit from megaplan side (already done):

| Site | Line | Symbol |
|---|---|---|
| `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` | 26, 36, 290-291 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB`, `hermes_cli.config.get_env_path` |
| `megaplan/review/parallel.py` | 80-81, 209-210 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB` |
| `megaplan/parallel_critique.py` | 51-52 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB` |
| `megaplan/workers.py` | 1580, 1649-1656 | `import run_agent` probe + `check_hermes_available()` |
| `megaplan/_core/io.py` | 270 | `import run_agent` probe |
| `tests/test_parallel_critique.py` | 404-407 | `sys.modules` fakes |
| `tests/test_parallel_review.py` | 198-201, 281-284 | `sys.modules` fakes |
| `README.md` | 23, 25, 83 | install doc |

All hermes imports in megaplan are lazy (inside functions) — `import megaplan.hermes_worker` does not pull hermes runtime deps, which makes the verify step cheap.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Pre-flight — clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (`git`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Stash/commit the three dirty files (`megaplan/execute/core.py`, `megaplan/prompts/execute_doc.py`, `megaplan/prompts/planning.py`) so subtree merge starts clean.
2. Record `git -C /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent rev-parse HEAD` and its default branch (assume `main`, fall back to `master`). Cite in the merge commit message for provenance.

### Step 2: Subtree-merge hermes-agent at `megaplan/agent/` (`git subtree`)
**Scope:** Small
1. From `megaplan` repo root: `git subtree add --prefix=megaplan/agent /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent main` (no `--squash` — brief says preserve history).
2. Confirm `megaplan/agent/run_agent.py` and `megaplan/agent/hermes_state.py` exist.
3. Ensure `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` is present (empty for Job A).

### Step 3: Map reachable-module surface inside the vendored tree (`megaplan/agent/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. BFS from `megaplan/agent/run_agent.py` and `megaplan/agent/hermes_state.py` via `grep -nE "^(from|import) " …` until only stdlib/third-party remain. This yields the "must-import-cleanly" set for Job A.
2. Tag transitive edges into `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `environments/` — their contents are Job B, but their absolute import paths need rewriting so the package loads.

### Step 4: Rewrite internal imports inside `megaplan/agent/` (`megaplan/agent/**/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. For every module reachable in Step 3, rewrite absolute sibling imports to the `megaplan.agent.*` namespace:
   - `from run_agent ...` → `from megaplan.agent.run_agent ...`
   - `from hermes_state ...` → `from megaplan.agent.hermes_state ...`
   - `from tools... / skills... / acp_adapter... / acp_registry... / environments...` → prefix with `megaplan.agent.`
   - `import run_agent` → `from megaplan.agent import run_agent`
2. Keep `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` empty (don't re-export) — preserves grep-ability at call sites. Explicit submodule imports in call sites is already megaplan's style.

### Step 5: Rewire megaplan call sites + remove `check_hermes_available` (`megaplan/*.py`, `tests/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Rewrite to vendored paths:
   - `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291` → `from megaplan.agent.run_agent import AIAgent` / `from megaplan.agent.hermes_state import SessionDB`.
   - `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81, 209-210` — same.
   - `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52` — same.
   - `megaplan/workers.py:1580` → `from megaplan.agent import run_agent`.
   - `megaplan/_core/io.py:270` → `from megaplan.agent import run_agent`.
2. Delete `check_hermes_available()` from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:23-49` (including its `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` call). Delete both call sites in `megaplan/workers.py:1649-1658` and collapse the surrounding branch to `raise CliError("agent_not_found", f"Agent '{agent}' not found")`.
3. Update test mocks: `tests/test_parallel_critique.py:404-407` and `tests/test_parallel_review.py:198-201, 281-284` — replace `sys.modules["run_agent"/"hermes_state"]` fakes with `monkeypatch.setattr(megaplan.agent.run_agent, "AIAgent", FakeAIAgent)` (vendored package now resolves normally; swapping the class is cleaner than module fakery).

### Step 6: Delete dead weight (`git rm` within `megaplan/agent/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `git rm -r` the brief's list: `evals/ landingpage/ website/ demo/ assets/ node_modules/ package.json package-lock.json tinker-atropos/ honcho_integration/ mini-swe-agent/ gateway/ cron/ hermes_cli/ cli.py auto_improve/ batch_runner.py rl_cli.py datagen-config-examples/ RELEASE_v*.md setup-hermes.sh cli-config.yaml.example`.
2. For "root *.json benchmark artifacts": enumerate with `git ls-files 'megaplan/agent/*.json'` (non-recursive), eyeball the list, remove only the benchmark outputs — do not recurse.
3. Leave `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `environments/` in place (Job B).

### Step 7: Packaging + README (`pyproject.toml`, `README.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Verify `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel] packages = ["megaplan"]` picks up `megaplan/agent` (it does — subpackage). Confirm the `exclude = ["megaplan/cloud/_reference/**"]` pattern doesn't accidentally cover `megaplan/agent`.
2. Update README line 23 from `pip install megaplan-harness hermes-agent` → `pip install megaplan-harness`. Leave the `~/.hermes/.env` note; the `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` env-var path in `megaplan/key_pool.py` still works without the deleted `hermes_cli.config.get_env_path`.
3. Do NOT pull hermes runtime deps into `pyproject.toml` in Job A — deferred (assumption below).

### Step 8: Verify imports and run the suite
**Scope:** Small
1. `python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io"` — must succeed with only megaplan's current deps.
2. `python -c "from megaplan.agent.run_agent import AIAgent; from megaplan.agent.hermes_state import SessionDB"` — must succeed once hermes runtime deps are present in the venv. Document any missing deps as a follow-up; do not bail out.
3. `grep -rE "^( *)(from|import) (run_agent|hermes_state|hermes_cli)" megaplan/ tests/` → zero hits outside `megaplan/agent/`.
4. `grep -r "check_hermes_available" megaplan/ tests/` → zero hits.
5. `pytest tests/` — green after test-mock updates in Step 5.
6. `megaplan --help` smoke.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (Step 1).
2. Subtree merge (Step 2) — creates vendored files.
3. Map reachable graph (Step 3) — gates the rewrite.
4. Rewrite internal imports (Step 4) — package must be importable before callers rewire.
5. Rewire megaplan call sites + strip gating + fix tests (Step 5).
6. Delete dead weight (Step 6).
7. Packaging + README (Step 7).
8. Validate (Step 8).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps (stale import paths, `check_hermes_available`).
2. Bare-Python import probe of megaplan modules.
3. Targeted pytest on the two touched test files.
4. Full `pytest tests/`.
5. `megaplan --help`.
6. (info) Manual hermes-backed phase run after runtime deps are installed.

        Requirements:
        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.
        - Update the plan to address the significant issues.
        - Keep the plan readable and executable.
        - Return flags_addressed with the exact flag IDs you addressed.
        - Include `changes_summary` as a short plain-English summary of what changed in the revision. If there were no concrete flags, say that explicitly (for example: `No critique flags were raised; refined wording and kept the plan aligned for execution.`).
        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

56	    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: float = _RELOAD_TTL_SECONDS) -> None:
57	        self._ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
58	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
59	        self._next_reload = 0.0
60	        self._hermes_env: dict[str, str] = {}
61	        self._entries: dict[str, list[KeyEntry]] = {provider: [] for provider in _PROVIDER_KEY_VARS}
62	    def _api_keys_path(self) -> Path:
63	        override = os.environ.get("MEGAPLAN_API_KEYS_PATH")
64	        if override:
65	            return Path(override).expanduser()
66	        repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
67	        candidates = (
68	            repo_root / "auto_improve" / "api_keys.json",
69	            repo_root.parent / "hermes-agent" / "auto_improve" / "api_keys.json",
70	        )
71	        for path in candidates:

73	                return path
74	        return candidates[0]
75	    def _load_hermes_env_unlocked(self) -> dict[str, str]:
76	        result: dict[str, str] = {}
77	        env_path = Path.home() / ".hermes" / ".env"
78	        if env_path.exists():
79	            for line in env_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
80	                line = line.strip()
81	                if line and not line.startswith("#") and "=" in line:
82	                    key, value = line.split("=", 1)
83	                    result[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip("'\"")
84	        return result
85	    def _collect_key_values(self, env_var: str, hermes_env: dict[str, str]) -> list[str]:
86	        values: list[str] = []
87	        seen: set[str] = set()
88	        names = _ENV_ALIASES.get(env_var, (env_var,))
89	        combined_names = set(hermes_env) | set(os.environ)
90	        for name in names:
91	            pattern = re.compile(rf"^{re.escape(name)}(?:_(\d+))?$")
92	            matches: list[tuple[int, str]] = []
93	            for candidate in combined_names:
94	                match = pattern.match(candidate)
95	                if match:
96	                    matches.append((int(match.group(1) or "1"), candidate))
97	            for _, candidate in sorted(matches):
98	                value = (os.environ.get(candidate) or hermes_env.get(candidate) or "").strip().strip("'\"")
99	                if value and value not in seen:
100	                    seen.add(value)
101	                    values.append(value)
102	        return values
103	    def _load_api_keys_json(self) -> list[str]:
104	        path = self._api_keys_path()
105	        if not path.exists():
106	            return []
107	        try:
108	            payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
109	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
110	            return []
111	        keys: list[str] = []
112	        if isinstance(payload, list):
113	            for item in payload:
114	                if isinstance(item, dict):
115	                    key = str(item.get("key", "")).strip()
116	                    if key and key not in keys:
117	                        keys.append(key)
118	        return keys
119	    def _load_keys_unlocked(self, now: float) -> None:
120	        if now < self._next_reload:
121	            return
122	        hermes_env = self._load_hermes_env_unlocked()
123	        self._hermes_env = hermes_env
124	        for provider, env_var in _PROVIDER_KEY_VARS.items():
125	            keys = self._collect_key_values(env_var, hermes_env)
126	            if provider == "zhipu":

130	            existing = {entry.key: entry for entry in self._entries.get(provider, [])}
131	            self._entries[provider] = [existing.get(key, KeyEntry(key=key)) for key in keys]

        # Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor the local `hermes-agent` repo into `megaplan` as an in-tree subpackage at `megaplan/agent/` using `git subtree` (history preserved), rewire megaplan's lazy imports from top-level `run_agent` / `hermes_state` to the vendored paths, delete the brief's dead-weight list **minus `hermes_cli/`**, remove the `check_hermes_available()` gating, repoint the sibling-repo fallbacks in `megaplan/key_pool.py` and `megaplan/workers.py`, add hermes runtime deps as an optional `[agent]` extra in `pyproject.toml`, and prove that every megaplan module still imports cleanly.

**Deviations from the brief (both driven by critique evidence, not preference):**
1. **Keep `hermes_cli/`** — `hermes-agent/run_agent.py:50` imports `hermes_cli.env_loader` and `hermes-agent/model_tools.py:181-182` imports `hermes_cli.plugins` at module-load time. Deleting it makes `megaplan.agent.run_agent` unimportable (critique verified via `sys.meta_path` blocking). Trim `hermes_cli/` in Job B alongside the other deferred directories.
2. **Fix two sibling-repo references that the brief did not list** — `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74` searches `repo_root.parent / "hermes-agent" / "auto_improve" / "api_keys.json"`, and `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665` tells users to "Install claude, codex, or hermes-agent." Leaving those in place would keep a live dependency on the external checkout, defeating the vendoring goal.

Reachable-surface audit from the megaplan side (already done — all hermes imports are lazy, so `import megaplan.hermes_worker` has no third-party cost):

| `megaplan/parallel_critique.py` | 51-52 | `AIAgent`, `SessionDB` |
